# 🎓 National Board of Examinations (NBE) Post-Graduate Question Papers Archive Index
This repository serves as a highly structured, fully categorized, and searchable database of **4139 official NBE medical question papers**. All links have been optimized to handle URL encoding (spaces replaced with `%20`) for seamless, direct-download access.
## 📊 Archive Statistics
| Metric | Count | Details |
| :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **Total Medical Specialties** | `72` | Fully categorized from broad and super specialties |
| **Total Scraped Question Papers** | `4139` | Complete database processed from Sheet1 |
| **DNB Diploma Papers** | `120` | Specialized PG diplomas started in **2021** |
| **DNB Broad Specialty Papers** | `3466` | Post-graduate core specialties (default broad board) |
| **DrNB Super Specialty Papers** | `553` | Post-doctoral super specialty and 6-year direct courses |
| **URL Standard Status** | `100% Clickable` | All blank spaces resolved to `%20` for instant access |

## 📌 NBE Classification Rules
To ensure structural accuracy and ease of reference, the database was parsed according to the following official board guidelines:
1. **DNB Diploma**: All records containing the word `Diploma` in their raw title are labeled as **DNB Diploma** (commenced in 2021).
2. **DNB Broad Specialties**: Non-diploma broad specialties. If "DNB" is not written in older titles (e.g., `PHYSIOLOGY-DEC12`), they are correctly mapped to **DNB** as the diploma courses did not exist before 2021.
3. **DrNB Super Specialties**: Records indicating Doctorate of National Board (`DrNB`) are labeled as **DrNB** (e.g. Cardiology, Neurosurgery).
